thi-ap1:0.2	Sumedhāvagga
thi-ap1:0.3	Sumedhātherīapadāna
thi-ap1:1.1	Namo tassa Bhagavato Arahato Sammāsambuddhassa.
thi-ap1:2.1	Atha therikāpadānāni suṇātha—
thi-ap1:3.1	“Bhagavati koṇāgamane,
thi-ap1:3.2	Saṅghārāmamhi navanivesanamhi;
thi-ap1:3.3	Sakhiyo tisso janiyo,
thi-ap1:3.4	Vihāradānaṁ adāsimha.
thi-ap1:4.1	Dasakkhattuṁ satakkhattuṁ,
thi-ap1:4.2	Dasasatakkhattuṁ satānañca satakkhattuṁ;
thi-ap1:4.3	Devesu upapajjimha,
thi-ap1:4.4	Ko vādo mānuse bhave.
thi-ap1:5.1	Deve mahiddhikā ahumha,
thi-ap1:5.2	mānusakamhi ko vādo;
thi-ap1:5.3	Sattaratanamahesī,
thi-ap1:5.4	itthiratanaṁ ahaṁ bhaviṁ.
thi-ap1:6.1	Idha sañcitakusalā,
thi-ap1:6.2	susamiddhakulappajā;
thi-ap1:6.3	Dhanañjānī ca khemā ca,
thi-ap1:6.4	ahampi ca tayo janā.
thi-ap1:7.1	Ārāmaṁ sukataṁ katvā,
thi-ap1:7.2	sabbāvayavamaṇḍitaṁ;
thi-ap1:7.3	Buddhappamukhasaṅghassa,
thi-ap1:7.4	niyyādetvā samoditā.
thi-ap1:8.1	Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi,
thi-ap1:8.2	tassa kammassa vāhasā;
thi-ap1:8.3	Devesu aggataṁ pattā,
thi-ap1:8.4	manussesu tatheva ca.
thi-ap1:9.1	Imasmiṁyeva kappamhi,
thi-ap1:9.2	brahmabandhu mahāyaso;
thi-ap1:9.3	Kassapo nāma gottena,
thi-ap1:9.4	uppajji vadataṁ varo.
thi-ap1:10.1	Upaṭṭhāko mahesissa,
thi-ap1:10.2	tadā āsi narissaro;
thi-ap1:10.3	Kāsirājā kikī nāma,
thi-ap1:10.4	bārāṇasipuruttame.
thi-ap1:11.1	Tassāsuṁ satta dhītaro,
thi-ap1:11.2	rājakaññā sukhedhitā;
thi-ap1:11.3	Buddhopaṭṭhānaniratā,
thi-ap1:11.4	brahmacariyaṁ cariṁsu tā.
thi-ap1:12.1	Tāsaṁ sahāyikā hutvā,
thi-ap1:12.2	sīlesu susamāhitā;
thi-ap1:12.3	Datvā dānāni sakkaccaṁ,
thi-ap1:12.4	agāreva vataṁ cariṁ.
thi-ap1:13.1	Tena kammena sukatena,
thi-ap1:13.2	cetanāpaṇidhīhi ca;
thi-ap1:13.3	Jahitvā mānusaṁ dehaṁ,
thi-ap1:13.4	tāvatiṁsūpagā ahaṁ.
thi-ap1:14.1	Tato cutā yāmamagaṁ,
thi-ap1:14.2	tatohaṁ tusitaṁ gatā;
thi-ap1:14.3	Tato ca nimmānaratiṁ,
thi-ap1:14.4	vasavattipuraṁ tato.
thi-ap1:15.1	Yattha yatthūpapajjāmi,
thi-ap1:15.2	puññakammasamohitā;
thi-ap1:15.3	Tattha tattheva rājūnaṁ,
thi-ap1:15.4	mahesittamakārayiṁ.
thi-ap1:16.1	Tato cutā manussatte,
thi-ap1:16.2	rājūnaṁ cakkavattinaṁ;
thi-ap1:16.3	Maṇḍalīnañca rājūnaṁ,
thi-ap1:16.4	mahesittamakārayiṁ.
thi-ap1:17.1	Sampattimanubhotvāna,
thi-ap1:17.2	devesu mānusesu ca;
thi-ap1:17.3	Sabbattha sukhitā hutvā,
thi-ap1:17.4	nekajātīsu saṁsariṁ.
thi-ap1:18.1	So hetu ca so pabhavo,
thi-ap1:18.2	Tammūlaṁ sāsane khamaṁ;
thi-ap1:18.3	Paṭhamaṁ taṁ samodhānaṁ,
thi-ap1:18.4	Taṁ dhammaratāya nibbānaṁ.
thi-ap1:19.1	Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṁ,
thi-ap1:19.2	bhavā sabbe samūhatā;
thi-ap1:19.3	Nāgīva bandhanaṁ chetvā,
thi-ap1:19.4	viharāmi anāsavā.
thi-ap1:20.1	Svāgataṁ vata me āsi,
thi-ap1:20.2	buddhaseṭṭhassa santike;
thi-ap1:20.3	Tisso vijjā anuppattā,
thi-ap1:20.4	kataṁ buddhassa sāsanaṁ.
thi-ap1:21.1	Paṭisambhidā catasso,
thi-ap1:21.2	vimokkhāpi ca aṭṭhime;
thi-ap1:21.3	Chaḷabhiññā sacchikatā,
thi-ap1:21.4	kataṁ buddhassa sāsanaṁ”.
thi-ap1:22.1	Itthaṁ sudaṁ sumedhā bhikkhunī imā gāthāyo abhāsitthāti.
thi-ap1:23.1	Sumedhātheriyāpadānaṁ paṭhamaṁ.

thi-ap6:1.1	“Nagare bandhumatiyā,

thi-ap6:2.3	‘Ādāya gamanīyaṁ hi,
thi-ap6:2.4	kusalaṁ natthi me kataṁ.
thi-ap6:3.1	Mahābhitāpaṁ kaṭukaṁ,
thi-ap6:3.2	ghorarūpaṁ sudāruṇaṁ;

thi-ap6:6.3	Jālena pidahitvāna,
thi-ap6:6.4	vatthayugena chādayiṁ.

thi-ap6:7.2	sarāmi yāvajīvihaṁ;

thi-ap6:7.4	tāvatiṁsamagacchahaṁ.
thi-ap6:8.1	Tiṁsānaṁ devarājūnaṁ,
thi-ap6:8.2	mahesittamakārayiṁ;
thi-ap6:8.3	Manasā patthitaṁ mayhaṁ,
thi-ap6:8.4	nibbattati yathicchitaṁ.
thi-ap6:9.1	Vīsānaṁ cakkavattīnaṁ,
thi-ap6:9.2	mahesittamakārayiṁ;
thi-ap6:9.3	Ocitattāva hutvāna,
thi-ap6:9.4	saṁsarāmi bhavesvahaṁ.
thi-ap6:10.1	Sabbabandhanamuttāhaṁ,
thi-ap6:10.2	apetā me upādikā;

thi-ap7:1.1	“Piṇḍacāraṁ carantassa,
thi-ap7:1.2	tissanāmassa satthuno;
thi-ap7:1.3	Kaṭacchubhikkhaṁ paggayha,
thi-ap7:1.4	buddhaseṭṭhassa dāsahaṁ.
thi-ap7:2.1	Paṭiggahetvā sambuddho,
thi-ap7:2.2	tisso lokagganāyako;
thi-ap7:2.3	Vīthiyā saṇṭhito satthā,
thi-ap7:2.4	akā me anumodanaṁ.
thi-ap7:3.1	‘Kaṭacchubhikkhaṁ datvāna,
thi-ap7:3.2	tāvatiṁsaṁ gamissasi;
thi-ap7:3.3	Chattiṁsadevarājūnaṁ,
thi-ap7:3.4	mahesittaṁ karissasi.
thi-ap7:4.1	Paññāsaṁ cakkavattīnaṁ,
thi-ap7:4.2	mahesittaṁ karissasi;
thi-ap7:4.3	Manasā patthitaṁ sabbaṁ,
thi-ap7:4.4	paṭilacchasi sabbadā.
thi-ap7:5.1	Sampattiṁ anubhotvāna,
thi-ap7:5.2	pabbajissasikiñcanā;
thi-ap7:5.3	Sabbāsave pariññāya,
thi-ap7:5.4	nibbāyissasināsavā’.
thi-ap7:6.1	Idaṁ vatvāna sambuddho,
thi-ap7:6.2	tisso lokagganāyako;
thi-ap7:6.3	Nabhaṁ abbhuggamī vīro,
thi-ap7:6.4	haṁsarājāva ambare.
thi-ap7:7.1	Sudinnaṁ me dānavaraṁ,
thi-ap7:7.2	suyiṭṭhā yāgasampadā;
thi-ap7:7.3	Kaṭacchubhikkhaṁ datvāna,
thi-ap7:7.4	pattāhaṁ acalaṁ padaṁ.
thi-ap7:8.1	Dvenavute ito kappe,
thi-ap7:8.2	yaṁ dānamadadiṁ tadā;
thi-ap7:8.3	Duggatiṁ nābhijānāmi,
thi-ap7:8.4	bhikkhādānassidaṁ phalaṁ.
thi-ap7:9.1	Kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṁ,
thi-ap7:9.2	…pe…
thi-ap7:9.3	viharāmi anāsavā.
thi-ap7:10.1	Svāgataṁ vata me āsi,
thi-ap7:10.2	…pe…
thi-ap7:10.3	kataṁ buddhassa sāsanaṁ.

thi-ap7:11.2	…pe…

thi-ap8:0.1	Therīapadāna
thi-ap8:0.2	Sumedhāvagga
thi-ap8:0.3	Sattuppalamālikātherīapadāna
thi-ap8:1.1	“Nagare aruṇavatiyā,
thi-ap8:1.2	aruṇo nāma khattiyo;
thi-ap8:1.3	Tassa rañño ahuṁ bhariyā,
thi-ap8:1.4	vāritaṁ vārayāmahaṁ.
thi-ap8:2.1	Satta mālā gahetvāna,
thi-ap8:2.2	uppalā devagandhikā;
thi-ap8:2.3	Nisajja pāsādavare,
thi-ap8:2.4	evaṁ cintesi tāvade.
thi-ap8:3.1	‘Kiṁ me imāhi mālāhi,
thi-ap8:3.2	sirasāropitāhi me;
thi-ap8:3.3	Varaṁ me buddhaseṭṭhassa,
thi-ap8:3.4	ñāṇamhi abhiropitaṁ’.
thi-ap8:4.1	Sambuddhaṁ paṭimānentī,
thi-ap8:4.2	dvārāsanne nisīdahaṁ;
thi-ap8:4.3	‘Yadā ehiti sambuddho,
thi-ap8:4.4	pūjayissaṁ mahāmuniṁ’.
thi-ap8:5.1	Kakudho vilasantova,
thi-ap8:5.2	migarājāva kesarī;
thi-ap8:5.3	Bhikkhusaṅghena sahito,
thi-ap8:5.4	āgacchi vīthiyā jino.
thi-ap8:6.1	Buddhassa raṁsiṁ disvāna,
thi-ap8:6.2	haṭṭhā saṁviggamānasā;
thi-ap8:6.3	Dvāraṁ avāpuritvāna,
thi-ap8:6.4	buddhaseṭṭhamapūjayiṁ.
thi-ap8:7.1	Satta uppalapupphāni,
thi-ap8:7.2	parikiṇṇāni ambare;
thi-ap8:7.3	Chadiṁ karonto buddhassa,
thi-ap8:7.4	matthake dhārayanti te.
thi-ap8:8.1	Udaggacittā sumanā,
thi-ap8:8.2	vedajātā katañjalī;
thi-ap8:8.3	Tattha cittaṁ pasādetvā,
thi-ap8:8.4	tāvatiṁsamagacchahaṁ.
thi-ap8:9.1	Mahānelassa chādanaṁ,
thi-ap8:9.2	dhārenti mama muddhani;
thi-ap8:9.3	Dibbagandhaṁ pavāyāmi,
thi-ap8:9.4	sattuppalassidaṁ phalaṁ.
thi-ap8:10.1	Kadāci nīyamānāya,
thi-ap8:10.2	ñātisaṅghena me tadā;
thi-ap8:10.3	Yāvatā parisā mayhaṁ,

thi-ap18:0.1	Therīapadāna
thi-ap18:0.2	Ekūposathikavagga
thi-ap18:0.3	Khemātherīapadāna
thi-ap18:1.1	“Padumuttaro nāma jino,
thi-ap18:1.2	sabbadhammesu cakkhumā;

thi-ap18:3.2	assosiṁ dhammadesanaṁ;

thi-ap40:16.1	Pesalātheriyāpadānaṁ dasamaṁ.
thi-ap40:16.2	Khattiyāvaggo catuttho.
thi-ap40:17.1	Tassuddānaṁ
thi-ap40:18.1	Khattiyā brāhmaṇī ceva,
thi-ap40:18.2	tathā uppaladāyikā;
thi-ap40:18.3	Siṅgālamātā sukkā ca,
thi-ap40:18.4	abhirūpā aḍḍhakāsikā.
thi-ap40:19.1	Puṇṇā ca ambapālī ca,
thi-ap40:19.2	pesalāti ca tā dasa;
thi-ap40:19.3	Gāthāyo dvisatānettha,
thi-ap40:19.4	dvicattālīsa cuttari.
thi-ap40:20.1	Atha vagguddānaṁ
thi-ap40:21.1	Sumedhā ekūposathā,
thi-ap40:21.2	kuṇḍalakesī khattiyā;
thi-ap40:21.3	Sahassaṁ tisatā gāthā,
thi-ap40:21.4	sattatālīsa piṇḍitā.
thi-ap40:22.1	Saha uddānagāthāhi,
thi-ap40:22.2	gaṇitāyo vibhāvibhi;
thi-ap40:22.3	Sahassaṁ tisataṁ gāthā,
thi-ap40:22.4	sattapaññāsameva cāti.
thi-ap40:23.1	Therikāpadānaṁ samattaṁ.
thi-ap40:24.1	Apadānapāḷi samattā.
